(91 Reviews)

tab\_Details:  

TRUE


custom\_fit:  

Regular


fabric:  

Mid weight, 5.3 oz, 28-singles, 100% combed cotton (heathers 15% viscose)


construction:  

Neck ribbing, side seamed, shoulder to shoulder tape, double needle hems, preshrunk to minimise shrinkage


printing\_techniques:  

Suited for screen printing, DTG and embroidery – [Click here](https://ascolour.com/embellishment-faqs) for more info


tab\_Shipping and Returns:  

TRUE


shipping\_chunk-1:  

We ship nationwide in the United States and Canada. Most in stock &amp; credit-approved orders received by 2pm local time where order is being shipped from, will be processed the same day.


shipping\_chunk-2:  

There may be a slight delay during sale periods due to high volumes. For more info see our [shipping](https://ascolour.com/shipping) page.

- Free shipping for orders over $500 (Wholesale) and $125 (Retail)



returns\_chunk-1:  

If you're not satisfied with your purchase, please return the items for an exchange or credit.


returns\_chunk-2:  

All returns must be made within 30 days of the invoice date with a completed returns form - for full terms and conditions see our [returns](https://ascolour.com/returns) page.


tab\_Care Instructions:  

Please note Army/Charcoal/Coal can be susceptible to fading/colouration variance. Also, particular sunblocks or deodorants mixed with high levels of sun exposure/body sweat can cause bleaching of the dyes in these colours - please consider.


care\_chunk-1:  

- Machine wash cold with like colours.
- Do not bleach.
- Do not tumble dry.
- Do not dry clean.
- Do not iron if printed.
- Line dry in shade.


care\_chunk-2:  

- Made in Bangladesh.

The AS Colour Staple Tee. Enduring comfort in a regular fit, crafted from 5.3 oz 100% combed cotton. Featuring neck ribbing, side seams, and double needle hems for durability and minimal shrinkage, it's a timeless choice for comfort and style. Available in 70+ colours. Built to last.

The AS Colour Staple Tee. Enduring comfort in a regular fit, crafted from 5.3 oz 100% combed cotton. Featuring neck ribbing, side seams, and double needle hems for durability and minimal shrinkage, it's a timeless choice for comfort and style. Available in 70+ colours. Built to last.

Description![](https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-hsi95a83fz/stencil/2aafa820-87e9-013f-3aa7-1251f161c312/e/a586c620-ed94-013e-d5ab-6e5098ba7f22/icons/caret-down.svg)

The AS Colour Staple Tee. Enduring comfort in a regular fit, crafted from 5.3 oz 100% combed cotton. Featuring neck ribbing, side seams, and double needle hems for durability and minimal shrinkage, it's a timeless choice for comfort and style. Available in 70+ colours. Built to last.

Details![](https://cdn11.bigcommerce.com/s-hsi95a83fz/stencil/2aafa820-87e9-013f-3aa7-1251f161c312/e/a586c620-ed94-013e-d5ab-6e5098ba7f22/icons/caret-down.svg)

Fit

Regular

Fabric

Mid weight, 5.3 oz, 28-singles, 100% combed cotton (heathers 15% viscose)

Construction

Neck ribbing, side seamed, shoulder to shoulder tape, double needle hems, preshrunk to minimise shrinkage

Embellishment

Suited for screen printing, DTG and embroidery – [Click here](https://ascolour.com/embellishment-faqs) for more info

Find a printer/embroider near you [here](https://ascolour.com/printers-embroiderers/)
